## Minute 0, pass 3

**Input**


**Tool `writeBrain`**

```json
{
  "text": "Basics (game 1 draft, from A01):\n1. Placement phase: take junction points with 4 neighbours (5, 11, 14, 20, 8, 17, 2, 10, 15, 23) before corners. Keep pieces spread so they can form mills on several lines.\n2. Always check first: can we close a mill this turn? Then can the opponent close one next turn? If opponent has two in a row with an empty third point, block it unless we mill first.\n3. Make double threats: place a piece that creates two open two-in-a-rows at once.\n4. Mill removal: remove an opponent piece that is part of a pending two-in-a-row threat, or a piece not in a mill. Pieces in mills are safe unless all are in mills.\n5. Movement phase: keep pieces mobile; avoid moves that leave us with no legal move. Open a mill by moving out and back in for repeated captures when the opponent cannot block.\n6. Endgame with 3 pieces: jumping is allowed; look for a jump that completes a mill while blocking the opponent's.\n7. Track the 40-turn no-mill draw clock; if behind, force trades; if ahead, avoid repetition.\nReview after each game and update with concrete lessons on the computer's habits."
}
```

-> Your team's brain is saved as version 1 (201 words).

2. Always check first: can we close a mill this turn? Then can the opponent close one next turn? If opponent has two in a row with an empty third point, block it unless we mill first.

5. Movement phase: keep pieces mobile; avoid moves that leave us with no legal move. Open a mill by moving out and back in for repeated captures when the opponent cannot block.
6. Endgame with 3 pieces: jumping is allowed; look for a jump that completes a mill while blocking the opponent's.
